INTRODUCTION

This document subscriber in detail the API (parameters,..) for interaction between Relying Party with GoPaperless Workflow Service. Besides, this document doesn’t contain the script for creating the Database GoPaperless Workflow.

Target

The document is suitable for customers/companies whose would like to interact with the GoPaperless Workflow Service.

Abbreviation

Notation Description
API Application Programming Interface
RSSP Remote Signing Service Provider
M Mandatory parameter
O Optional parameter
RC Required Condition
RSA Rivest-Shamir-Adleman
ECDSA Elliptic Curve Digital Signature Algorithm
DSA Digital Signature Algorithm
OSIANA Operating SystemInternet Assigned Numbers Authority

System Connection Information

The following table lists connection parameters used to build API endpoint URLs.

No Name Value Description
1 domain prd-gopaperless.mobile-id.vn Current production domain
2 contextPath /workflow/api API context path
3 baseUrl https://{domain}/{contextPath}/XXX Template for API endpoint URLs

API Specification

All of the APIs in this document are HTTP web service RESTful.

Authenticate – Log in

Client Credentials Authentication

This function is responsible for retrieving an access token, which is required for subsequent function calls.

The client must provide credentials issued by the service provider (GoPaperless Workflow Service). The server will authenticate these credentials and, if valid, return an access token for use in subsequent API requests.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticateContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "grant_type": "client_credentials", "client_id": "Application_name", "client_secret": "client_secret", "remember_me_enabled": true}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticateContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "grant_type": "client_credentials", "client_id": "Application_name", "client_secret": "client_secret", "remember_me_enabled": true}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "access_token": "Token", "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": 300, "refresh_token": "refreshtoken", "refresh_token_expires_in": 1800}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "access_token": "Token", "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": 300, "refresh_token": "refreshtoken", "refresh_token_expires_in": 1800}

Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 grant_type String M Defines the type of access token to be returned (following OAuth 2.0). Supported value: "client_credentials".
2 client_id String M The Application Name that was configured in your Enterprise Account during the integration setup.
3 client_secret String M The API key which be generated in Enterprise Account.
4 remember_me_enabled Boolean O If this parameter is true (enabled) => GoPaperless Workflow Service will return a refresh token embedded into the response. Default is false.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 access_token String M The access token will be used later to connect with other APIs.
5 token_type String M Type of the token returned. It is always set to “bearer”.
6 expires_in Integer M Number of seconds for which this access token is valid.
7 refresh_token String M This is a token related to access token. Can use this token to revoke.
8 refresh_token_expires_in Integer M Number of seconds for which this refresh token is valid.

Password Authentication

This function retrieves an access token, which will be used in subsequent API calls.

The client must provide user credentials issued by the service provider (GoPaperless Workflow Service). The server will validate the provided credentials and return an access token if they are correct.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticateContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "grant_type": "password", "client_id": "Application_name", "client_secret": "client_secret", "username": "admin@mobile-id.vn", "password": "OneTwoThreeFour", "scope": "giatk@mobile-id.vn", "remember_me_enabled": true}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticateContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "grant_type": "password", "client_id": "Application_name", "client_secret": "client_secret", "username": "admin@mobile-id.vn", "password": "OneTwoThreeFour", "scope": "giatk@mobile-id.vn", "remember_me_enabled": true}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "access_token": "Token", "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": 300, "refresh_token": "refreshtoken", "refresh_token_expires_in": 1800}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "access_token": "Token", "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": 300, "refresh_token": "refreshtoken", "refresh_token_expires_in": 1800}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 grant_type String M Define which type of access token will be returned (through the OAuth 2.0): + “password” + “refresh_token”
2 client_id String M Application Name that you have configured in your Enterprise Account when configuring the Integration options.
3 client_secret String M The API key which be generated in Enterprise Account.
4 username String M GoPaperless Workflow email address identifying the target user account - this can be an Enterprise Admin or an Enterprise User account. Note an enterprise user can use an API Key created for the enterprise by an enterprise administrator.
5 password String M Password for the account defined in the 'username' parameter.
6 remember_me_enabled Boolean O If this parameter is true (enabled) => GoPaperless Workflow Service will return a refresh token embedded into the response. Default is false.
7 scope String O Used when the Enterprise Admin user is identified in 'username' and you wish to identify an Enterprise User as the target. That is, set an enterprise user as the current user for operations.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 access_token String M The access token will be used later to connect with other APIs.
5 token_type String M Type of the token returned. It is always set to “bearer”.
6 expires_in Integer M Number of seconds for which this access token is valid.
7 refresh_token String M This is a token related to access token. Can use this token to revoke.
8 refresh_token_expires_in Integer M Number of seconds for which this refresh token is valid.

Single Sign On Authentication

This function retrieves an access token, which will be used in subsequent API calls.

A business application can call this API to obtain a user's access token by making a request to a third-party service, based on the provided method and token.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticate/ssoContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "token": "String", "code": "String", "method": "String", "profile_name": "String", "profile_id": 0, "remember_me_enabled": true}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticate/ssoContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "token": "String", "code": "String", "method": "String", "profile_name": "String", "profile_id": 0, "remember_me_enabled": true}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "access_token": "Token", "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": 300, "refresh_token": "refreshtoken", "refresh_token_expires_in": 1800}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "access_token": "Token", "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": 300, "refresh_token": "refreshtoken", "refresh_token_expires_in": 1800}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 token String M Token obtained from 3rd party (Ex: JWT,..)
2 code String M
3 method String M Supported method type: + “OAUTH2”
4 profile_name String M Authentication profile name, required for "OAUTH2" and "OIDC"
5 profile_id Integer M Authentication profile id, Its an alternate of profile_name
6 remember_me_enabled Boolean O If this parameter is true (enabled) => GoPaperless Workflow Service will return a refresh token embedded into the response. Default is false.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 access_token String M The access token will be used later to connect with other APIs.
5 token_type String M Type of the token returned. It is always set to “bearer”.
6 expires_in Integer M Number of seconds for which this access token is valid.
7 refresh_token String M This is a token related to access token. Can use this token to revoke.
8 refresh_token_expires_in Integer M Number of seconds for which this refresh token is valid.

OTP Login Authentication

This function retrieves an access token, which will be used in subsequent API calls.

GoPaperless Workflow supports two-factor authentication (2FA) using a One-Time Password (OTP) during login. To enable OTP-based authentication, the Enterprise Administrator must configure this option in the Enterprise Role settings.

At least one SMS provider must be configured in the GoPaperless Workflow Service to use this API.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticate/otpContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "mobile_number": "String", "method": "SMS"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticate/otpContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "mobile_number": "String", "method": "SMS"}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "access_token": "Token", "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": 300, "refresh_token": "refreshtoken", "refresh_token_expires_in": 1800}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "access_token": "Token", "token_type": "bearer", "expires_in": 300, "refresh_token": "refreshtoken", "refresh_token_expires_in": 1800}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 mobile_number String M Define the mobile number of user. The OTP will be send to Application.
2 method String M Method possible values are: + SMS + EMAIL
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 access_token String M The access token will be used later to connect with other APIs.
5 token_type String M Type of the token returned. It is always set to “bearer”.
6 expires_in Integer M Number of seconds for which this access token is valid.
7 refresh_token String M This is a token related to access token. Can use this token to revoke.
8 refresh_token_expires_in Integer M Number of seconds for which this refresh token is valid.

Revoke refresh token

This function revokes the refresh tokens issued for a user.

A refresh token is generated and sent when an authentication request is successfully processed.

Sample Request

DELETE https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/tokensContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "refresh_token": "refresh_token_of_accesstoken", "token_type_hint": "refresh_token"}DELETE https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/tokensContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: en{ "refresh_token": "refresh_token_of_accesstoken", "token_type_hint": "refresh_token"}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 refresh_token String M OAuth refresh access token to revoke
2 token_type_hint String M Type of token. Currently only “refresh_token” is supported
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.

Get Information

This function retrieves information about GoPaperless Workflow Services.

Additionally, this API can be used to verify the validity of an access token by including it in the request header.

Sample Request

GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/infoContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"{}GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/infoContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"{}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "gopaperless.workflow.app.langs": "en;vn", "gopaperless.workflow.app.name": "GoPaperless Workflow Services", "gopaperless.workflow.app.dateformat": "dd/MM/yyyy", "gopaperless.workflow.app.region": "region1", "gopaperless.workflow.app.logo": "https://gopaperless.workflow.mobile-id.vn", "gopaperless.workflow.app.methods": [“info”,“authenticate”,“tokens”,“workflow"] "gopaperless.workflow.app.version": "23.02.24", "gopaperless.workflow.app.authType": [“basic”,“oauth2code"] "gopaperless.workflow.app.description": "GoPaperless Workflow is a smart workflow management"}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "gopaperless.workflow.app.langs": "en;vn", "gopaperless.workflow.app.name": "GoPaperless Workflow Services", "gopaperless.workflow.app.dateformat": "dd/MM/yyyy", "gopaperless.workflow.app.region": "region1", "gopaperless.workflow.app.logo": "https://gopaperless.workflow.mobile-id.vn", "gopaperless.workflow.app.methods": [“info”,“authenticate”,“tokens”,“workflow"] "gopaperless.workflow.app.version": "23.02.24", "gopaperless.workflow.app.authType": [“basic”,“oauth2code"] "gopaperless.workflow.app.description": "GoPaperless Workflow is a smart workflow management"}

No Name Type Presence Description

Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.

Pre Login Authentication

This function retrieves the authentication method for the user based on the configuration set in the enterprise role.

Sample Request

GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticate/preContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "email": "String"}GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/authenticate/preContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "email": "String"}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "profile_id": 0, "profile_name": "String", "method": "String", "app_id": "String", "tenant_id": "String", "url": "String", "scope": "String", "resource ": "String"}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "profile_id": 0, "profile_name": "String", "method": "String", "app_id": "String", "tenant_id": "String", "url": "String", "scope": "String", "resource ": "String"}

Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description

Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description

Response Attributes

4 status String M Status of the user. Possible values are+ NOT_FOUND+ ACTIVE

Document Package

Pre Upload Document

Business Applications can use this service API to pre check a document before call subsequence APIs.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/documents/preContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "file_data": "Base64 of file"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/documents/preContent-Type: application/octet-streamAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{File(binary)}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/documents/preContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "file_data": "Base64 of file"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/documents/preContent-Type: application/octet-streamAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{File(binary)}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "document_status": 0, "document_status_name": "This document exists an PdfFormField"}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "document_status": 0, "document_status_name": "This document exists an PdfFormField"}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 file_data String RC (If content-type is application/json) Data of the file in Base64 format.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 document_status Integer M Status of the document. Possible values are: + 0 : Exist a Form Field + 1 : Document is empty
5 document_status_name String M Name of the status

Add Document Package

After authenticating into GoPaperless Workflow Service (e.g., Business Application), this mandatory function must be called first to create a package in GoPaperless Workflow.

This API is used to create a blank package containing one or more documents. Additionally, the package can be used to add fields, data, and other elements to initiate a new workflow.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packagesContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "package_name": "String"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packagesContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "package_name": "String"}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "package_id": 0}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "package_id": 0}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 package_name String M Defines the name of the package.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 package_id Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.

Share Document Package/ Share Workflow

Business applications can use this API to share a document package with signers and initiate a new workflow.

Additionally, the document package must be prepared using the Preparation API before sharing it with signers.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/workflow/shareContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/workflow/shareContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{}

Upload Document

The client can use this API to add a document to a document package, which is linked to a user account. The document metadata is sent in the HTTP header, while the document data (binary) is sent in the HTTP body.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documentsContent-Type: application/octet-streamAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-file-name: "Name of file"x-language-name: en{ File(binary)}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documentsContent-Type: application/octet-streamAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-file-name: "Name of file"x-language-name: en{ File(binary)}

Sample Response

Http Code: 201Content-Type: application/json{ "documentid": 0}Http Code: 201Content-Type: application/json{ "documentid": 0}

Upload Document (base64)

The client can use this service API to add a document to a document package, which is linked to a user account. The document metadata is sent in the HTTP headers, while the document data (Base64-encoded) is sent in the HTTP body

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/base64Content-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-file-name: "Name of file"x-language-name: en{ "document": "Base64"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/base64Content-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-file-name: "Name of file"x-language-name: en{ "document": "Base64"}

Download Package

Business applications can use this service API to download a document package in binary format. If the package contains a single document, the downloaded file will be a binary PDF. However, if the package includes multiple documents, the download will be a binary ZIP file containing all documents.

Note: The parameters x-password and x-otp are required if the package owner (the user who created the package) has enabled this option during the workflow creation phase.

Sample Request

GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}Content-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-password: "password123"x-otp: "otp"{}GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}Content-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-password: "password123"x-otp: "otp"{}

Sample Response

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
3 x-password String O Password of the document
4 x-otp String O OTP value for opening the document
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 Byte[] M File data in binary format

Update Document

Business applications can use this service API to update a document of a document package. The document information is sent in the HTTP request header and document bytes are sent in the HTTP request body.

GoPaperless Workflow supports a wide variety of document formats, each of which can be converted to PDF format upon upload.

Note PDF documents are not altered upon upload to the system.

Note a package must already exist before you can add a document using this call.

Note this feature is supported only for PDF documents.

Sample Request

PUT https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/updateContent-Type: application/octet-streamAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-file-name: "Test.pdf"{ File(binary)}PUT https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/updateContent-Type: application/octet-streamAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-file-name: "Test.pdf"{ File(binary)}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
3 x-file-name String O Name of the document.
4 Content-Type String M Using “application/octet-stream” to upload your document with binary format.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
2 documentId Integer M The document ID is required for sharing, downloading, or appending the document to a workflow.
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 Byte[] M Binary of the document
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.

3 Content-Type String M Using “application/octet-stream” to upload your document with binary format.
4 x-append-to-top Boolean O True if the file should be prepended to the document. Default is false
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
2 documentId Integer M The document ID is required for sharing, downloading, or appending the document to a workflow.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.

Apply Workflow Template

Business applications can use this service API to apply a workflow template to a document. The document ID on which template has to be applied is provided in the resource URL.

While applying the template it is important to remember these two important points:

Point 1 - If the template being applied is created using a PDF which already contained form fields then ensure that the document on which this template is now being applied must contain these form fields in advance and GoPaperless Workflow will NOT create those form fields via the template rather only apply the form data and assign to the respective users.

Point 2 - If the template being applied is created where form fields are manually added (hence not present in the PDF originally) via GoPaperless Workflow e.g. signature field, initial, in-person signature, check boxes, radio buttons, text fields etc. then on applying such a template, these form fields will be created on the target document even if these form fields were already present in the document.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/templateContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "template_name": "String", "apply_to_all": false}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/templateContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "template_name": "String", "apply_to_all": false}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "document_id": 0, "document_name": "Name", "document_order": 0, "document_type": "PDF", "document_width": 100, "document_height": 100, "document_pages": 1, "form_fields": true, "lock_form_fields": true, "created_at": "date", "modified_at": "date", "template": { "template_name": "String", "read_only": true }}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "document_id": 0, "document_name": "Name", "document_order": 0, "document_type": "PDF", "document_width": 100, "document_height": 100, "document_pages": 1, "form_fields": true, "lock_form_fields": true, "created_at": "date", "modified_at": "date", "template": { "template_name": "String", "read_only": true }}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Add Electronic Seal to Workflow

Business applications can use this service API to add an Electronic Seal recipient to a workflow. This call will only add a recipient to the workflow. That is, no fields currently on the document will be automatically assigned to the new Electronic Seal recipient(s), nor will this create any new fields. Note the Electronic Seal recipient(s) will be added as the last person in the workflow. Hence further work may be required to remove a current Electronic Seal recipient(s) already present in the workflow. At least one recipient must exist in a workflow before fields can be added to the document. Note: the input accepts one or more Electronic Seal recipient(s) in a single call. Note: only PDF type document is supported.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/workflow/electronic-sealsContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}" x-language-name: en{ "name": "String", "signing_order": 0}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/workflow/electronic-sealsContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}" x-language-name: en{ "name": "String", "signing_order": 0}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "name": "String", "signing_order": 0, "level_of_assurance": "String"}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "name": "String", "signing_order": 0, "level_of_assurance": "String"}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

2 signing_order Integer M Order in which the workflow will be signed by the recipients. This signing order is important when workflow type is set to "CUSTOM".

Add Placeholder to Workflow

Business applications can use this service API to add placeholder to a workflow in a package. A place holder is required when you do not know the identity of the intended recipient in a workflow.

Note the placeholder will be added as the last user in the workflow.Hence further work may be required to remove a current user/group, already present in the workflow.

Adding a place holder allows you to proceed and add signature and/or input fields to the document, which can then be assigned to individual users at a later stage. While XML type document preparation, only supported role types are "SIGNER", "REVIEWER" and "CARBON_COPY".

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/workflow/placeholderContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "placeholders": [ { "placeholder": "PlaceHolder Name", "email_notification": true, "role": "SIGNER", "signing_order": 1 } ]}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/workflow/placeholderContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "placeholders": [ { "placeholder": "PlaceHolder Name", "email_notification": true, "role": "SIGNER", "signing_order": 1 } ]}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "placeholders": [ { "placeholder": "PlaceHolder Name", "order": 1 } ]}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{ "placeholders": [ { "placeholder": "PlaceHolder Name", "order": 1 } ]}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 placeholders List<addPlaceholderRequest> M Arrays of the Placeholder
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 placeholders List<addPlaceholderResponse> M List of Placeholders

Update Placeholder in Workflow

Business applications can use this service API to update a placeholder in the workflow. Normally this call is useful after a template has been applied to a document and business applications wants to override the details of a specific place holder within the workflow. The ID of the workflow document is provided in the resource URL, and the workflow place holder is identified by the order at which it is added to the workflow. While XML type document preparation, only supported role types are "SIGNER", "REVIEWER" and "CARBON_COPY".

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/workflow/{order}/placeholderContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "placeholder": "PlaceHolder Name", "email_notification": true, "role": "SIGNER", "signing_order": 1}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/workflow/{order}/placeholderContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "placeholder": "PlaceHolder Name", "email_notification": true, "role": "SIGNER", "signing_order": 1}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{}Http Code: 200Content-type: application/json{}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
2 order Integer M Order id of the placeholder
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 placeholder String M Placeholder name
2 email_notification Boolean M If set as true, GoPaperless Workflow will send notifications to the user via email as per the document owner and user notification settings. A value of false means no notifications will be sent to user throughout the workflow.
3 role String M Role of the participant. Possible values are here
4 signing_order Integer M Order of the recipient in the workflow. This signing order is mandatory when workflow type is "CUSTOM".
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description

3 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description

PUT https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/initialsContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-dimension-unit: "percentage" x-language-name: en{ "page": 1, "field_name": "Init", "renamed_as": "Init2", "dimension": { "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100 }}PUT https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/initialsContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-dimension-unit: "percentage" x-language-name: en{ "page": 1, "field_name": "Init", "renamed_as": "Init2", "dimension": { "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100 }}

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 x-dimension-unit String O The unit of the dimension. Possible values are: + percentage + point (default)
3 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description

2 documentId Integer M The document ID to be used to share, download or append it into workflow
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 renamed_as String O If this parameter is not null => the field_name will be updated
2 field_name String M Name of the field.
3 page Integer O Page to define where the field embedded at.
4 dimension dimension O Dimension of the field.
5 visible_enabled Boolean O Show the field if this flag is true.
6 image String O Image data of the Initial field.
7 required Object O The object to show required in your application.
8 suffix String O Suffix of the field.
9 remark Object O Remark in your application
10 replicate_all_pages Boolean O True if the client wants to replicate the initial field on all pages
11 apply_to_all Boolean O True if the client wants to set the image of this initial field for all initial fields in the workflow.
12 replicate_pages List<Integer> O List the page numbers to which the client wants this initial field to be replicated.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.

Assign Document Field

Business applications can use this service API to assign user to the field (QR field, Digital Signature Field, …). The field must be already be present in the document and the users must be present in the workflow. This API call allows you to assign a user by their workflow order number, to an input field on the document.

Sample Request

PUT https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v4/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/assignContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "fields_assign": [ { "field_name": "FieldA", "radio_group_name": "Radio Group", "order": 1 }, {} ]}PUT https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v4/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/assignContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "fields_assign": [ { "field_name": "FieldA", "radio_group_name": "Radio Group", "order": 1 }, {} ]}

Add In-person Field

Business applications can use this service API to an add in-person signature field to a document in a package.

At least one user must exist in a workflow before fields(input and signature) can be added to the document in this way.The order number corresponds to the recipient in the workflow.Therefore, this number must be equal or less than the total number of users in the workflow. The below example assigns this new signature field to the first person in the workflow.

See Add Users to Workflow for more information. Note if you have a document with a template applied, or have applied one using the APIs, then the workflow will already contain users.

In order to host an in-person meeting a GoPaperless Workflow user must be part of the workflow.They must either be added with Role "INPERSON_HOST" or "SIGNER". For the host option they do not require a signature or input field on the document, and the in-person signature field can be signed by the non-GoPaperless Workflow registered user.

Note this is a special workflow scenario whereby it is expected that the GoPaperless Workflow user and recipient would be in the same location.This type of signature is not intended for use whereby a document is shared with recipients over email link in the normal loose integration method.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/in_person_signatureContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-dimension-unit: "percentage"{ "order": 1, "field_name": "signature 1", "page": 1, "dimension": { "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100 }, "visible_enabled": true, "level_of_assurance": [ "ELECTRONIC_SIGNATURE" ]}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/in_person_signatureContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-dimension-unit: "percentage"{ "order": 1, "field_name": "signature 1", "page": 1, "dimension": { "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100 }, "visible_enabled": true, "level_of_assurance": [ "ELECTRONIC_SIGNATURE" ]}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "field_name": "String"}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "field_name": "String"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 x-dimension-unit String O The unit of the dimension. Possible values are: + percentage + point (default)
3 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
2 documentId Integer M The document ID to be used to share, download or append it into workflow
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 page Integer M Page number at which the field is about to be created.
2 field_name String M Name of the field that is to be added. If not provided, system will assign an unique auto generated name to the field.
3 dimension dimension M Location of the field
4 visible_enabled Boolean M True if the field is visible
5 level_of_assurance List<String> O Top location of the field in pixel. Level Of Assurance to be added. Possible Values are here
6 authentication_signing FieldAuthentication O Field Authentication data
7 verification verificationSignatureRequest O Data of the signature field to define more information (lock field, …)
8 required Object O The object to show required in your application.
9 suffix String O Suffix of the field.
10 remark Object O Remark in your application
11 annotation_position String O Position of the signature. Possible values are here
12 appearance_design String O The appearance of Signature frame. Possible values are here
13 order Integer M Order id of the participant.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 field_name String M Name of the field

Update In-person Field

Business applications can use this service API to update an in-person signature field of a document in a package.

Note all of the input parameters can be changed.Where you wish to retain the current setting submit it in the call unchanged.You can therefore use the same parameters as when you added the input field, or the information as returned by Get Document Fields.The latter can be used when a template was applied or the document was created using the GUI interface.

Sample Request

PUT https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/in_person_signatureContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-dimension-unit: "percentage"{ "order": 1, "field_name": "signature 1", "page": 1, "dimension": { "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100 }, "visible_enabled": true, "level_of_assurance": [ "ELECTRONIC_SIGNATURE" ]}PUT https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/in_person_signatureContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-dimension-unit: "percentage"{ "order": 1, "field_name": "signature 1", "page": 1, "dimension": { "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100 }, "visible_enabled": true, "level_of_assurance": [ "ELECTRONIC_SIGNATURE" ]}

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/checkboxContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-dimension-unit: "percentage"{ "field_name": "DateTime 1", "page": 1, "type": "TEXTBOX", "value": "String", "max_length": 0, "format": "String", "format_type": "Number", "multiline": true, "font": { "name": "Arial", "size": 12, "embedded_size": "String" }, "dimension": { "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100 }}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/documents/{documentId}/fields/checkboxContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-dimension-unit: "percentage"{ "field_name": "DateTime 1", "page": 1, "type": "TEXTBOX", "value": "String", "max_length": 0, "format": "String", "format_type": "Number", "multiline": true, "font": { "name": "Arial", "size": 12, "embedded_size": "String" }, "dimension": { "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100 }}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "field_name": "String"}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "field_name": "String"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 x-dimension-unit String O The unit of the dimension. Possible values are: + percentage + point (default)
3 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
2 documentId Integer M The document ID to be used to share, download or append it into workflow
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 page Integer M Page number at which the field is about to be created.
2 field_name String M Name of the field that is to be added. If not provided, system will assign an unique auto generated name to the field.
3 dimension dimension M Location of the field
4 visible_enabled Boolean M True if the field is visible
5 read_only Boolean O The flag to define if the field can be interact. Default is true
6 color String O Color of the text. Possible values are here
7 align_option alignmentOption O Alignment object to show the position of the text.
8 value String RC(Required attribute for processing.) Data of the field.
9 group_name String O Name of the group (one group can have multiple checkbox field).
10 check_box_type String O Type of the checkbox. Possible values are: + CHECK/Check/check + CIRCLE/Circle/circle + CROSS/Cross/cross + DIAMOND/Diamond/diamond + SQUARE/Square/square + STAR/Star/star.
11 checked Boolean O The flag to define if the checkbox field is checked.
12 font font O Font of the text in the field.
13 place_holder String O Place holder of the field.
14 tool_tip_text String O To show tool tip text of the field.
15 multiple_checked Boolean O The flag to define if the field can be have more than 2 check in one group.
16 required Object O The object to show required in your application.
17 suffix String O Suffix of the field.
18 remark Object O Remark in your application
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 field_name String M Name of the field

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 x-dimension-unit String O The unit of the dimension. Possible values are: + percentage + point (default)

No Name Type Presence Description

8 remark Object O Remark in your application
9 is_transparent Boolean O The flag to define if the QR Code background is transparent.
10 image_qr String RC(Required attribute for processing.) Image data of the QR Code (when you have and qr image, just send it with this parameter).
11 value String RC(Required attribute for processing.) The data of QR Code.Note: If you send data in String, the GoPaperless Workflow Service will generate QRCode based on your data.
12 allow_unauthenticated_workflow_view Boolean O If this flag is enabled, the GoPaperless Workflow will generate a UUID. This UUID will then be combined with a custom URL (configured in Enterprise Settings or provided as a parameter) and embedded within the QR code.
13 custom_url String O This custom URL provides an alternative for the client to define the target URL, instead of using the URL configured within the Enterprise Settings.

No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.

Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description

2 documentId Integer M The document ID to be used to share, download or append it into workflow

No Name Type Presence Description
1 page Integer M Page number at which the field is about to be created.
2 field_name String M Name of the field that is to be added. If not provided, system will assign an unique auto generated name to the field.
3 dimension dimension M Location of the field
4 visible_enabled Boolean M True if the field is visible
5 read_only Boolean O The flag to define if the field can be interact. Default is true
6 required Object O The object to show required in your application.
7 suffix String O Suffix of the field.
8 remark Object O Remark in your application
9 color String O Color of the text. Possible values are here
10 align_option alignmentOption O Alignment object to show the position of the text.
11 value String RC(Required attribute for processing.) Data of the field.
12 multiline Boolean O The flag to define if the field can have multiple line. Default is false.
13 max_length Integer O The number of the length in the field.
14 format_type String O Format type of the field. Possible values are: + ALPHANUMERIC + NUMBER + TEXT + EMAIL + TIME + DATE
15 font font O Font of the text in the field.
16 place_holder String O Place holder of the field.
17 tool_tip_text String O To show tool tip text of the field.
18 address String RC(Required attribute for processing.) Data of the hyperlink field

No Name Type Presence Description

2 hand_signature_image String O Base64 Encoded string image of the visible signature appearance
3 signing_algo String M The algorithm to be signed. Possible values are here
4 contact_information String O The contact name of the signature
5 hashing_algo String M The algorithm to hash data to be sign. Possible values are here
6 signing_reason String O The reason of the signature
7 signing_location String O The location of the signature
8 skip_verification Boolean O If true, show the verification of signature when processed successfully
9 certificate_chain List<String> M The array list of certificate chain. Format like this:[ Certificate_User, //Must contain Certificate_CA1, //Option Certificate_RootCA //Option]
10 signing_order Integer M Signing Order of the User
11 time_zone String O Timezone in IANA format.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 hash String M Data of the hash
5 state String M Unique alphanumeric value per transaction
6 hashing_algo String M Oid of hashing algorithm using which document hash is computed

Submit Document

One of the GoPaperless Workflow roles is called "Editor". This allows a recipient to edit input fields on a document, but does not require a signature. Once completed the recipient submits their changes. The button used in the GUI is called "Submit".

This is the equivalent operation for an editor to submit their changes via an API call.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/submitContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-password: "password"x-otp: "otp"{}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/packages/{packageId}/submitContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-password: "password"x-otp: "otp"{}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-password String O Password of the workflow
2 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
3 x-otp String O OTP of the workflow
4 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 x-client-ip String O The IP address of the client (this parameter will be displayed in the TSE application).
3 x-os-name String O The operating system of the client (this parameter will be displayed in the TSE application).
4 x-browser-name String O The browser name of the client (this parameter will be displayed in the TSE application).
5 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
2 documentId Integer M The document ID to be used to share, download or append it into workflow
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 field_name String M Field name to determine which field will be processed
2 hand_signature_image String O Base64 Encoded string image of the visible signature appearance
3 signing_algo String M The algorithm to be signed. Possible values are here
4 signer_contact String O The contact name of the signature
5 hashing_algo String M The algorithm to hash data to be sign. Possible values are here
6 signing_reason String O The reason of the signature
7 signing_location String O The location of the signature
8 skip_verification Boolean O If true, show the verification of signature when processed successfully
9 credential_id String M Credential id of the signer. Get it from “Get Filtered Credential List”
10 appearance_design String O The appearance of Signature frame. Possible values are here
11 connector_name String M Name of the Connector that returned in API “Get RSSP Information”
12 time_zone String O Timezone in IANA format.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 validity_period String RC(When the credential’s mode is TSE) The validity period of a TSE defines the timeframe during which the device is authorized to operate and generate valid fiscal data.
5 vc_code String RC(When the credential’s mode is TSE) The unique, randomly generated string that is used to identify and track a specific transaction.

Enterprise Management

Register Enterprise User

Business Applications can use this service API to register Enterprise User. New users inherit the Enterprise Admin Service Plan and all associated permissions. And there are different scenarios to consider when registering a user.

Users will get an “account activation” email on successful registration, if password, security question and answer parameters are not provided in the call. Email notification flag would be ignored by the GoPaperless Workflow.

Users will get an “account activation” email on successful registration,if password, security question and answer parameters are provided in the call. Email notification flag should be set to True

Users will not get any email on successful registration, if password, security question and answer parameters are provided in the call => Email notification flag set to False

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/enterprise/usersContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "user_name": "String", "user_email": "String", "user_password": "String", "security_question": "String", "security_answer": "String", "enterprise_role": "String", "email_notification": true, "certificate_id": 1, "job_title": "String", "company_name": "String", "mobile_number": "String", "country": "String", "time_zone": "String", "language": "String", "user_national_id": "String", "common_name": "String"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/enterprise/usersContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "user_name": "String", "user_email": "String", "user_password": "String", "security_question": "String", "security_answer": "String", "enterprise_role": "String", "email_notification": true, "certificate_id": 1, "job_title": "String", "company_name": "String", "mobile_number": "String", "country": "String", "time_zone": "String", "language": "String", "user_national_id": "String", "common_name": "String"}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi

Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 user_name String M The name of the user to be registered.
2 user_email String M The email address of the user account to be registered.
3 user_password String M The password of the user account
4 security_question String M Security question used to reset password if user forgets his password.
5 security_answer Boolean O Security answer used to reset the password if user forgets his password.
6 enterprise_role String M The enterprise role to be assigned to this user
7 email_notification String M This defines whether enterprise user would get an email notification about the account creation. Default value is 'false'.
8 certificate_id String O Certificate ID
9 job_title String O Job title of user
10 company_name String O Company name of user

Update Workflow User Permissions of Enterprise Package

Business Applications can use this service API to update the document permissions for a User in that workflow. This API can also be used by the enterprise administartor.

Get Workflow User Authentication(Document Opening) of Enterprise Package

Business Applications can use this service API to get the access security settings enabled for the package. Recipients will not be able to access document package outside the scope of this duration if the access security is enabled. Document owner can also configure authentication based security of the package for a recipient

Sample Request

GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/enterprise/packages/{packageId}/workflow/{order}/authenticationContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{}GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/enterprise/packages/{packageId}/workflow/{order}/authenticationContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 x-search-text String O Search text if required. This is optional and without it the entire list is returned.Furthermore, recommended value for search text parameter is in Base64 encoded format.
3 x-enterprise String O If set as "true" only enterprise groups list will be returned. In case of "false"only the user's groups list will be returned.If you do not set the header, then both enterprise and user's groups lists will be returned.
4 x-total-records String O Total number of records found with the provided search criteria.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 recordPerPage String M
2 pageNo String M
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description

2 pageNo Integer O Page number, according the division of records perpage
3 recordsPerPage Integer O Number of records that are needed to be fetched in one request
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 groups List<group> M The array of groups

Add Identity for a User

By default, the application identifies users using their email address. To provide more flexibility, clients can assign a unique identifier to a user via the API below. Currently, only identity verification through the VNeID service is supported.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/account/identityContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "sync_user_from_connector": "NameOfConnector", "webhook_url": "Your url"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/account/identityContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "sync_user_from_connector": "NameOfConnector", "webhook_url": "Your url"}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "message": "PROCESSING", "validity_period": 120}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "message": "PROCESSING", "validity_period": 120}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + VI
2 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 sync_user_from_connector String M The name of the connector through which the user wishes to synchronize identity information.
2 webhook_url String M The webhook URL where you want to receive status updates.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 validity_period Integer M The validity period during which the processing request remains active.

3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 address_books List<addressBook> M An array that representing the address book that belongs to the user.

Get Templates

This API allows business applications to retrieve a list of templates for the current user. You can search these templates by providing criteria under the 'x-search-text' header.

The API also supports searching for personal templates or enterprise-wide templates, depending on the user's role and permissions granted by their enterprise administrator.

Sample Request

GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/settings/templates/{recordPerPage}/{pageNo}Content-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-search-text: "{base64}"x-enterprise: truex-total-records: 10{}GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/settings/templates/{recordPerPage}/{pageNo}Content-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-search-text: "{base64}"x-enterprise: truex-total-records: 10{}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "templates": [ { "template_id": 0, "template_name": "name", "template_public": true, "read_only": true, "created_at": "", "description": "String", "enterprise": false, "documents": [ { "id": 0, "name": "String" } ], "smart_form": { "active_count": 0, "total_count": 0 } } ]}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "templates": [ { "template_id": 0, "template_name": "name", "template_public": true, "read_only": true, "created_at": "", "description": "String", "enterprise": false, "documents": [ { "id": 0, "name": "String" } ], "smart_form": { "active_count": 0, "total_count": 0 } } ]}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 Authorization String M The authentication token (accessToken) required to access the API. Must be provided in the format “Bearer <accessToken>”Note: the accessToken is obtained after a successful authorization.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 packageId Integer M This is the ID of the package that was created. Using this ID to Interact with that document package.
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 name String M Name of the template that is to be added.
2 description String O Name of the description that is to be added.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 Id Integer M Id of the template

Application/Device Management

Register Application User

Business Applications can use this service API to register User that belongs to the application. New users inherit the User Service Plan and all associated permissions. And there are different scenarios to consider when registering a user.

Users will get an “account activation” email on successful registration, if password, security question and answer parameters are not provided in the call. Email notification flag would be ignored by the GoPaperless Workflow.

Users will get an “account activation” email on successful registration,if password, security question and answer parameters are provided in the call. Email notification flag should be set to True

Users will not get any email on successful registration, if password, security question and answer parameters are provided in the call => Email notification flag set to False

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/application/usersContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{"user_name": "String","user_email": "String","user_password": "String","security_question": "String","security_answer": "String","enterprise_role": "String","email_notification": true,"job_title": "String","company_name": "String","mobile_number": "String","country": "String","time_zone": "String","language": "String","user_national_id": "String","common_name": "String"//Identity data"full_name": " String ","user_national_id": "String","date_of_birth": "1990-01-15T08:30:00","gender": 1","permanent_address": " String ","residential_address": " String ","eid_account_level": 2,"account_type": " String ","date_of_issue": "2020-06-01T00:00:00","date_of_expiry": "2030-06-01T00:00:00"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/application/usersContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{"user_name": "String","user_email": "String","user_password": "String","security_question": "String","security_answer": "String","enterprise_role": "String","email_notification": true,"job_title": "String","company_name": "String","mobile_number": "String","country": "String","time_zone": "String","language": "String","user_national_id": "String","common_name": "String"//Identity data"full_name": " String ","user_national_id": "String","date_of_birth": "1990-01-15T08:30:00","gender": 1","permanent_address": " String ","residential_address": " String ","eid_account_level": 2,"account_type": " String ","date_of_issue": "2020-06-01T00:00:00","date_of_expiry": "2030-06-01T00:00:00"}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 accesstoken String M The token used to authorize into GoPaperless Workflow Service. It is response in API Authorize
2 user_first_name String M First name of the new user
3 user_last_name String M Last name of the new user
4 user_email String M The email address of the user account to be registered.
5 user_password String O The password of the user account
6 security_question String O Security question used to reset password if user forgets his password.
7 security_answer Boolean O Security answer used to reset the password if user forgets his password.
8 email_notification String O This defines whether enterprise user would get an email notification about the account creation. Default value is 'false'.
9 certificate_id String O Certificate ID
10 job_title String O Job title of user
11 company_name String O Company name of user
12 mobile_number String O Mobile number of user
13 country String O Country name for the user
14 time_zone String O Timezone name for the user
15 language String O Language selected for the user
16 user_national_id String O National identity number of the user

18 user_ra_id String O User ID of the user registered in SAM service inside SigningHub Engine (ADSS Server).
19 user_csc_id String O
20 common_name String O An identifiable name for the user that added as Common Name (CN) in identity certificate.
21 full_name String O Full legal name of the user.
22 date_of_birth String O User’s date and time of birth (ISO 8601 format).
23 gender Integer O User’s gender. Possible values are:1: MALE2: FEMALE
24 permanent_address String O User’s officially registered permanent address.
25 residential_address String O User’s current residential address.
26 eid_account_level Integer O User’s electronic ID account level.
27 account_type String O Type of user account (e.g., CITIZEN)
28 date_of_issue String O Date and time the ID or account was issued.
29 date_of_expiry String O Expiration date and time of the ID or account.
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
