Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "contacts": [ { "contact_id": 1, "user_first_name": "string", "user_last_name": "string", "user_email": "string" }, {} ]}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "contacts": [ { "contact_id": 1, "user_first_name": "string", "user_last_name": "string", "user_email": "string" }, {} ]}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 x-search-text String O Search text if required. This is optional and without it the entire list is returned.Furthermore, recommended value for search text parameter is in Base64 encoded format.
3 x-enterprise String O If set as "true" only enterprise groups list will be returned. In case of "false"only the user's groups list will be returned.If you do not set the header, then both enterprise and user's groups lists will be returned.
4 x-total-records String O Total number of records found with the provided search criteria.
Path Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 pageNo String M
2 recordPerPage String M
Request Attributes

2 pageNo Integer O Page number, according the division of records perpage
3 recordsPerPage Integer O Number of records that are needed to be fetched in one request
Response Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.
2 error_description String M Detailed description of the error.
3 message String M Human-readable error message.
4 contacts List<contact> M The array of the contact

Update Template

Business applications can use this service API to update personal/enterprise template. This API can also be used by the business application to perform action on behalf of the enterprise user via "Scope Authentication".

ADMIN APIs

Admin Authentication

This API can be used to authenticate to GoPaperless Workflow Service Administrator. It requires a current, valid client TLS certificate

The API call should present a client TLS certificate in the HTTPS call

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/admin/authenticate/client_certContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: enx-password: "String"{}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/admin/authenticate/client_certContent-Type: application/jsonx-language-name: enx-password: "String"{}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "access_token": "string", "token_type": "string", "expires_in": 0}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "access_token": "string", "token_type": "string", "expires_in": 0}

Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}Http Code: 401Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Invalid Request data", "error_code": "1101261", "message": "Your client_id is invalid. Please try again"}

Attributes description

No Name Type Presence Description

Create Account

This API can be used to create a new account in GoPaperless Workflow.

You must authenticate using your Paperless Workflow Administration client TLS certificate

The API call should present a client TLS certificate in the HTTPS call

Get Accounts

API is used to get accounts and filter them based on the search criteria.

Sample Request

GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/admin/accounts/{pageNo}/{recordsPerPage}Content-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-user-name: "String"x-user-email: "String"x-enterprise-name: "String"x-billing-mode: "String"x-payment-type: "String"x-service-plan: "String"x-activated: "String"x-enabled: "String"x-from: "String"x-to: "String"x-total-records: "String"{}GET https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/admin/accounts/{pageNo}/{recordsPerPage}Content-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: enx-user-name: "String"x-user-email: "String"x-enterprise-name: "String"x-billing-mode: "String"x-payment-type: "String"x-service-plan: "String"x-activated: "String"x-enabled: "String"x-from: "String"x-to: "String"x-total-records: "String"{}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "accounts": [ { "user_name": "string", "user_email": "string", "service_plan": "string", "enterprise_id": 0, "enterprise_name": "string", "created_on": "string" } ]}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{ "accounts": [ { "user_name": "string", "user_email": "string", "service_plan": "string", "enterprise_id": 0, "enterprise_name": "string", "created_on": "string" } ]}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

Header Request Attributes
No Name Type Presence Description
1 x-language-name String O Display the error message in the specified language. Possible values are: + en + vi
2 x-user-name String O Name of the account user to be searched
3 x-user-email String O Email of the account user to be searched.
4 x-enterprise-name String O Name of enterprise by which the account is to be searched.
5 x-billing-mode String O Billing mode for the account, possible values are ONLINE, OFFLINE. If developers intend to ignore billing mode, it's optional.
6 x-payment-type String O Payment type for the account search, possible values are PAY_REGULARLY, PAY_AS_YOU_GO.
7 x-service-plan String O Name of service plan to search
8 x-activated String O True, if accounts are activated.
9 x-enabled String O True, if accounts are enabled.
10 x-from String O Date value in ISO format from where the account is to be searched.
11 x-to String O Date value in ISO format to which the account is to be searched.
12 x-total-records String O Total number of records found with the provided search criteria.

No Name Type Presence Description
1 error_code String M Error code identifying the failure.

Service Plan Reset

Business applications can use this API to reset the Service Plan statistics for a user. Every user in GoPaperless Workflow has an associated Service Plan. Within this the number of signatures, workflows, templates, etc., are recorded. This API call allows the calling application to reset these statistics for the given user.

Sample Request

POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/admin/accounts/statisics/resetContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "user_email": "String"}POST https://{domain}/{contextPath}/v1/admin/accounts/statisics/resetContent-Type: application/jsonAuthorization: "Bearer {accesstoken}"x-language-name: en{ "user_email": "String"}

Sample Response

Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}Http Code: 200Content-Type: application/json{}

Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}Http Code: 400Content-type: application/json{ "error_description": "Cannot verify token", "error_code": "-17", "message": "Không thể kiểm tra tính hợp lệ của token"}

Attributes description

ERROR CODE HTTP

CODE MESSAGE DESCRIPTION
200 OK The request send successfully and get the response from server.
201 Created Your request has been created
400 Bad request Your request doesn’t contain enough values or your payload have a problem.
401 Unauthorized Didn’t authorized yet and call subsequence API.
403 Forbidden Error occurred by your account (permission,..) or processing the request fail.
404 Not found Your data request not found in Service.
500 Internal Server Error An Internal Server error occurred while processing the request

Workflow

Workflow
No Name Type Presence Description
1 workflow_type String M Type of the workflow, there are four types of workflows supported in the application. Possible values are "SERIAL", "PARALLEL", "INDIVIDUAL" and "CUSTOM".
2 continue_on_decline Boolean M Data verification of the field True, if workflow needs to be continued even after a recipient declines at his turn.
3 workflow_status String M Processing status of the workflow. Possible values are here
4 workflow_state String M State of the workflow (note based on user). Possible values are here
4 workflow_mode String M Mode of the workflow, there are three types of workflow modes supported in application. Possible values are "ONLY_ME", "ME_AND_OTHERS" and "ONLY_OTHERS"
5 message String M Any custom string message that is to be sent to all the recipient of the workflow by the document owner.
6 read_only Boolean M If any template applied on the documents in this workflow is read only or locked template then the workflow is read only. SigningHub will not allow the end users to make any changes to this workflow in draft state and this workflow is to be shared as it is.
7 post_process Object M
8 comments Boolean M If this workflow have an comment => True

10 category_id Integer O Id of the Category

If image exist it will be provided by hitting the defined url otherwise it will be an empty string.

15 identity_document_type String M Type of the identity document
16 email String M Email of the user
17 mobile String M Mobile number of the user
18 portrait String M Portrait image of the user

Items

Items
No Name Type Presence Description
1 field String M This parameter will define the name of the field
2 type Integer M This parameter will define the type of that field. Possible values are: + 1 : String (Text) + 2 : Boolean + 3 : Int + 4 : Date (TimeStamp.UNIX) + 5 : Binary / Base 64 (for file/image) + 6 : Text Bold + 7 : Choice + 8 : Table + 9 : File + 10: URL + 11: ID Picture with 4 Labels + 12: Non-Editable Element + 13: Empty**See this example for more details: example
3 value Object M Data of the field. The structure based on these example
4 remark String O Remark of the field
5 mandatory_enabled Boolean O The flag to ensure that field is mandatory

ENUMERATION

WorkflowMode

No Name Description
1 CUSTOM The workflow allows for asynchronous signing, where signers can sign at their convenience without adhering to a specific sequence.
2 PARALLEL The workflow enables concurrent signing, allowing multiple signers to sign simultaneously.
3 SERIAL The workflow requires sequential signing, with signers signing one after another in a predetermined order.
4 INDIVIDUAL None

WorkflowStatus

No Name Description
1 DRAFT A preliminary version of the workflow that is still under development or review.
2 PENDING The workflow is pending user action (signing, approval, etc.).
3 INPROGRESS The workflow is being processed by other participant.
4 COMPLETED The workflow has been fully executed and all required actions have been completed. All participants have signed and the workflow has reached its final state.
5 DECLINED The workflow has been rejected and will not proceed further.
6 EXPIRED This status indicates that the workflow has been prematurely ended due to an issue or problem.
7 SIGNED The workflow has been signed by this user
8 EXPIRING The workflow will expire in 7 days.

LevelOfAssurance

No Name Description
1 electronic signature An electronic signature is a digital signature that authenticates the identity of a person and their intent to approve or endorse an electronic document
2 electronic seal An electronic seal is a digital representation of an organization's official seal.
3 cades signature CAdES is a standard for advanced electronic signatures. It's a framework for creating and verifying digital signatures that provide a high level of security and legal validity. CAdES signatures can include additional attributes like timestamping, non-repudiation, and revocation information.

LockAction

No Name Description
1 include
2 exclude
3 all

LockPermission

No Name Description
1 FORM_FILLING_AND_ANNOTATION When enabled this type -> The document can only be signed with the Signature Field. Otherwise, can append more Annotation/Stamp/Widget into it (not make the signature invalid)
2 FORM_FILLING When enabled this type -> The document can only be signed with the Signature Field (cannot append any type of Annotation/Stamp/Widget)
3 NO_CHANGES_ALLOWED When enabled this type -> The document cannot be changed by any type of action. One little change will make the signature become into “invalid”

AnnotationPosition

No Name Description
1 FIRST_PAGE_TOP Signature will be placed at the top of the first page.
2 FIRST_PAGE_BOTTOM Signature will be placed at the bottom of the first page
3 LAST_PAGE_TOP Signature will be placed at the top of the last page.
4 LAST_PAGE_BOTTOM Signature will be placed at the bottom of the last page.
5 CUSTOM Signature will be placed based on the Dimensions

DocumentType

No Name Description
1 USERNAME Type USERNAME in RSSP
2 PERSONAL-ID Type PERSONAL-ID in RSSP
3 CITIZEN-IDENTITY-CARD Type CITIZEN-IDENTITY-CARD in RSSP
4 BUDGET-CODE Type BUDGET-CODE in RSSP
5 PASSPORT-ID Type PASSPORT-ID in RSSP
6 TAX-CODE Type TAX-CODE in RSSP
7 UUID Type UUID in RSSP

No Name Description
1 RSA RSA Signing Algorithm (Default)
2 ECDSA ECDSA Signing Algorithm
3 DSA DSA Signing Algorithm

No Name Description

AppearanceDesign

No Name Description
1 ADVANCED_ELECTRONIC_SIGNATURE Type of the credential is Advanced electronic signature

AppearanceAlignment

No Name Description
1 LEFT The text of appearance will align left
2 AUTO The text of appearance will align auto
3 RIGHT The text of appearance will align right

WorkflowState

No Name Description
1 DRAFT A preliminary version of the workflow that is still under development or review.
2 PENDING The workflow is waiting for the next action or approval.
3 INPROGRESS The workflow is currently being processed or executed.
4 COMPLETED The workflow has been fully executed and all required actions have been completed. All participants have signed and the workflow has reached its final state.
5 DECLINED The workflow has been rejected and will not proceed further.
6 TERMINATED This status indicates that the workflow has been prematurely ended due to an issue or problem.

Example

Items request

Type Example
1 - String { "items":[ { "field": "Text Field", "type": 1, "value": "Value of field" } ]}
2 - Boolean { "items":[ { "field": "Male", "type": 2, "value": true } ]}
3 - Number { "items":[ { "field": "Age", "type": 3, "value": 18 } ]}
4 - Date { "items":[ { "field": "Date start", "type": 4, "value": "2023-12-15" } ]}
5 – Base64(for file/image) { "items":[ { "field": "Photo", "type": 5, "value": "{Base_64_image}" } ]}
6 – Text Bold { "items":[ { "field": "Text Bold", "type": 6, "value": "Testing" //if null, the field name will be chosen } ]}
7 – Choice { "items":[ { "field": "Choice", "type": 7, "value": [ { "element": "Nhan vien", "choice": false },{ "element": "Truong Phong", "choice": true },{ "element": "Giam Doc", "choice": false } ] } ]}
8 – Table { "items":[ { "field": "Table", "type": 8, "value": [ //Row { "column_1": "A", "column_2": "B", "column_3": "C", "text": "mini text" } ] } ]}
9 – File { "items":[ { "field": "File1", "type": 9, "value": "{Base64_of_file}" "file_field": "randomString(1)", "file_format": "image/png", "file_name": "Hand.png" } ]}
10 - URL { "items":[ { "field": "Link GoPaperless", "type": 10, "value": { "url": "https://uat-portal.qrypto.ee/portal/login/sign-in", "label": "Website" } ]}
11 – ID Picture with 4 Labels { "items":[ { "field": "Photo", "type": 11, "value": { "label_1": "one", "label_2": "two", "label_3": "three", "label_4": "four", "file_data": "{Base64_of_file}" }, "file_field": "fileA", "file_format": "image/png", "file_name": "hello.png" } ]}
12 – Non-Editable Element { "items":[ { "field": "Non-Editable", "type": 12, "value": "Cannot edit this field" } ]}
13 - Empty
